- [ ] [Review][Patch] **`byManaValueThenName` has no final tiebreak.** Two rows with the same `cmc` and name (two printings of one card) keep wire order, which `schema.ts` calls "not meaningful". Their tiles can swap places between refetches, and `coldOpenTargetOf` can change. Add a `card_id` tiebreak [`ui/src/state/deckGroups.ts:238-239`].

**Rejected**

- `false`: a throwing `onStatus` or `onUpdate` skips `onReconnected`, leaves `emitted` committed undelivered, or kills the poll (two lenses, five findings). `onStatus` is `applyConnection`, a zustand `setState`, plus `refreshInstanceId` and `reconcileDeckBoot`, which never throw. The store's listeners are React's and `deck.ts:631`'s, which only assigns and re-drives. F2 rejected the same premise. The `finally` that already exists is defensive, and its missing test is the patch above.
- `false`: the store's status goes stale across `stop()` → `start()` (two lenses). `useAgentConnection` creates a fresh instance on every effect run, and nothing restarts a stopped instance. `App` is the one consumer and never unmounts in production, and StrictMode's cleanup runs before any socket opens.
- `false`: `refreshInstanceId` rejects on a rejecting injected reader (two lenses). The production reader, `readInstanceId`, is total, and only tests inject a reader.
- `false`: `deckOf` accepts a `cards` array holding `null` or card-less rows. The rows are Pydantic `DeckCardFull` from the same-origin backend, as F2 ruled.
- `false`: the `mergedSignal` fallback leaks listeners. The caller signal is a per-refetch controller (`deck.ts:378`), and the timeout listener is `once` and fires within 10 s.
- `false`: a row with no `cmc` sorts as `NaN`. `cmc` is a required `number` in the `Card` schema.
- `false`: `inspection.ts` lacks `__proto__` hardening. It stores ids as values and compares them by equality; it indexes no object by id.
- `false`: FR-14 and FR-15 disagree for `/health`. FR-14 is the backend's `instance_id` probe (the schema type), and FR-15 is the UI showing connection status (the refresh). Each comment cites the requirement it serves.
- `false`: `localeCompare` with no locale is nondeterministic. It uses the user's runtime locale, which is fixed within a tab.
- `low`: alternating `database_unavailable` and `unreachable` keeps the backoff at 2 s and the stalled clock reset. The reset on a network rejection is documented intent (`poller.ts:199`). A refusal is never cut off by the 10 s timeout, and a backend restart drops the socket, whose `restartPoll` resets everything anyway.
- `low`: `readFormatCheck` takes no `AbortSignal`, so a superseded check still costs a `get_deck_with_cards`. This is the 2026-09-03 audit's open P3 "UI abort signals" and F2's rejected boot-reads abort; the fix threads a controller through `App`'s effect. Merge in step 4.
- `low`: there is no connect timeout on a socket whose upgrade hangs. The mint is an HTTP request to the same process and fails first under `READ_TIMEOUT_MS`, and the fix adds a timer per attempt.
- `low`: there is no ping and no `visibilitychange`/`online` fast path. A dead loopback peer sends an RST, so `close` fires, and a resumed tab waits at most the 30 s ceiling.
- `low`: a burst of `deck_changed` frames restarts a stopped poll once per frame. That is one `GET /api/decks` per frame, which is the design (the deck list may have changed), and the fix exposes poller state.
- `not a defect`: the extra focus areas live mostly outside F1's files (intent-alignment). Request dedupe is in `cards.ts`, the per-detail format-check trigger in `App.tsx`, and the panel decisions in `components/StatePanel/states.ts`. Noted for step 4; F3 and F8 cover the latter two.
- `not a defect`: `formatCheck.fixtures.ts` is test-support data under `src/state/`. Only test files import it, so it is not bundled.
- `not a defect`: `systemState.ts` has no colocated suite. It is exercised through `App.test.tsx`, which T1 reviews.

Image throughput is the backend pacer's (B12). The thumbnail request patterns of the agent views run through F4's `useCardArt`, `cardImageUrl` and `frontFaceCost`, but they live in F5–F7.
- **What holds:**
  - **AD-11:** every image goes through `cardImageUrl` to `/api/card-image/`, and `tests/no-scryfall-hosts.test.ts` still guards it. A failed face draws the named placeholder, never a broken-image glyph, and the two faces fail independently.
  - **AD-12 hydration owner:** the panel's `hydrateCard` is the group's only component-started fetch, and `cards.ts` dedupes in-flight reads and terminal refusals. Deck cards arrive seeded `hydrated`, so the grid fetches no records.
  - **Hover cost:** `useIsLiveTarget` returns a boolean per tile, so a hover re-renders the two tiles whose ring changed, not the grid.
  - **Validity:** the flip control is a sibling of the tile's `<button>`, never inside it. `useCardArt` re-arms during render on a key change, so a stale `load` cannot mark the next card shown.
  - **Split costs:** `frontFaceCost` splits before taking a cost verbatim, so no `' // '` reaches `ManaCost`. `cards.db` has 0 rows with an empty half.

- [ ] [Review][Decision] **The detail panel starts a `size=large` fetch for every card the cursor crosses.** `src` binds straight to the inspection target (`CardDetail.tsx:496`, and `:516` for the back face of every flippable card, flipped or not). A sweep across a cold 99-tile grid starts up to 99 large requests (plus one per DFC). Each is a disk-cache miss that queues in the same backend pacer (`Semaphore(4)`, 0.1 s spacing) as the grid's own cold `normal` images. So a cursor moving during cold open slows the grid's first paint. The header's "each distinct card is fetched at most once" covers `hydrateCard` only, not the art. Options:
  - (a) Hover intent: set the large `src` only once the target has held for about 150 ms. Meanwhile show the tile's `normal` URL, which the grid already has in the browser cache.
  - (b) Always show the `normal` URL first and swap to `large` on settle, without a delay.
  - (c) Mount the back face only after the first flip in the panel, alone or with (a) or (b).
  - (d) Leave it, since AD-11 fetches only what the tab asks for and the pacer bounds the CDN rate.

  Recommended: (a). It removes the sweep storm with no new request, and the panel never shows an empty well because the `normal` render is warm. (c) trades the warm flip for a cold fetch at the rotation midpoint, which the tile rejected for the same reason.
- [ ] [Review][Decision] **Comments cite deleted gates.** 41 citations in this group name 9 `ui/tests` files that commit `e584980` deleted: `shell.test.ts` (13), `token-usage.test.ts` (9), `posture.test.ts` (5), `store-writes.test.ts` (3), `wire-contract.test.ts` (3), `empty-deck-copy.test.ts` (3), `copy-rules.test.ts` (2), `pin-announcement-copy.test.ts` (2) and `quantity-glow.test.ts` (1). Only `no-scryfall-hosts.test.ts` (2) survives. They describe as gated:
  - the `CARD_SHAPED` split behind the three paired stylesheets (`CardTile.css`/`QuantityBadge.css`, `CardDetail.css`/`CardDetailChrome.css`, `FlipControl.css`);
  - the one-door fetch posture (`useCardArt.ts`'s "`posture.test.ts`'s one-door list");
  - the store-write ban (`CardTile.tsx:298`, `CardDetail.tsx:350`);
  - the static flip label's attribute rule (`FlipControl.tsx`, `copy-rules.test.ts`);
  - the three `copy.ts` headers' "NO IMPORTS is load-bearing", which existed for `ui/tests` importers that are gone.

  Options: (a) reword each as a stated convention, naming what enforces it today, if anything; (b) restore the cheap halves as lint rules, then (a); (c) delete the citations. Recommended: the same ruling as F1 and F3. Here that is (a), plus the empty-deck literal test (patch below).
- [ ] [Review][Decision] **Every flip control has the same accessible name.** `aria-label="Flip card"` is static on purpose (`FlipControl/copy.ts`, and no card data in a read-aloud attribute). But in a screen reader's button list on a deck with several DFCs, every entry reads "Flip card, toggle button", and the panel's copy reads the same as the tile's [`ui/src/containers/FlipControl/FlipControl.tsx:110`]. Options:
  - (a) Add `aria-describedby`, pointing at the tile's caption id or the panel's name element. That needs a new optional prop on `FlipControl`.
  - (b) Keep it, relying on DOM order (the control follows its tile).

  Recommended: (a). The label stays static, the card data stays out of the copy module, and the description gives each control its card.
- [ ] [Review][Decision] **A sideboard-only deck renders an empty list with no explanation.** `deckIsEmpty` is false for it, so `CardGrid` renders an empty `<ul>`. That announces "list, 0 items", which the header argues against, and the screen says nothing. The header calls it a named residue with no live population [`ui/src/containers/CardGrid/CardGrid.tsx:84-85`, `:126`]. Options:
  - (a) Add a sideboard-only line to EXPERIENCE.md and `CardGrid/copy.ts`.
  - (b) Render no `<ul>` when there are no tiles, and add no copy.
  - (c) Leave it.

  Recommended: (b). It removes the "0 items" announcement for the cost of one condition. The deck list beside the grid already shows the sideboard.
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **Tabbing from a tile to its own flip control drops the inspection target for one commit.** `onBlur={() => clearFocused(cardId)}` and `onFocus` on the frame are separate dispatches. A user-initiated focus move runs a microtask checkpoint between `focusout` and `focusin`, so React commits the cleared state:
  - the panel renders the hover or cold-open card;
  - its `useCardArt` re-arms to `'loading'` on the way back, so the panel's art flashes the well.

  The comments in `CardTile.tsx:356-362` and the test name claim the bubbling "keeps the tile's inspection target". `CardTile.test.tsx:669-686` checks only the end state. Skip the clear when `relatedTarget` is inside the frame. Test that the target never leaves the card across the move, for example with a store subscription that records every value [`ui/src/containers/CardTile/CardTile.tsx:364-370`]
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **Esc while focus is on the Unpin button drops focus to `<body>`.** The document listener calls `clearPin()`, while the click path calls `releaseAndRefocus`. The Unpin button renders only while pinned, so Esc removes the focused element, and Tab restarts from the top of the page. This is the failure the click path's comment exists to prevent. When focus is inside the frame, Esc should hand it to the panel heading as the click does. Add a test in `CardDetail.test.tsx` [`ui/src/containers/CardDetail/CardDetail.tsx:375-385`, `:407-410`]
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **The pin announcement names the front face while the panel shows the flipped one.** The capture calls `contentOf(readCardEntry(pinnedId))` with the default face 0. Deck cards are seeded `hydrated` (`cards.ts:194-208`), so `fromCard(card, 0)` returns `card_faces[0].name`. Pin a DFC flipped to its back, and the heading shows the back face while the live region says "Pinned — <front face>". The caption shows a third name, the combined one. The comment at `:310-314` blames the summary tier's combined name, which applies only to an unhydrated id. Read the pinned id's face index at capture, and correct the comment [`ui/src/containers/CardDetail/CardDetail.tsx:310-321`]
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **The empty-deck sentence is pinned only against itself.** `CardGrid/copy.ts:8` and `:18-19` say `tests/empty-deck-copy.test.ts` parses the EXPERIENCE.md cell. That file is deleted. `CardGrid.test.tsx:200` and four `App.test.tsx` sites compare the rendered text with `EMPTY_DECK_LINE` itself, so rewording the sentence or losing the U+2014 passes every test. Add one rendered-output assertion against the literal, as F3's attribution patch does. The pin announcement already has one (`CardDetail.test.tsx:567`) [`ui/src/containers/CardGrid/CardGrid.test.tsx:200`]
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **No test shows the detail panel's art as loaded.** Visibility depends on `data-loaded={frontArt === 'shown' …}` and `onLoad={onFrontLoad}` (and the back-face pair), and the image stays at `opacity: 0` until then. `CardDetail.test.tsx` fires only `error`, never `load`, and never reads `data-loaded` or checks for the well. Dropping `onLoad`, or binding the front's `data-loaded` to `backArt`, would hide every panel image and pass the suite. Mirror `CardTile.test.tsx:200-206` on `.is-front`, and add a load on `.is-back` to the flipped-face block [`ui/src/containers/CardDetail/CardDetail.tsx:481`, `:495`, `:508`, `:515`, `:519`]
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **`settleIfCached` is never called by a test, and the comments say it can't be.** It is the only way out of `'loading'` for an image the browser finished before React listened, which is the warm-grid path. `useCardArt.test.ts:28-33`, the header at `useCardArt.ts:65-71` and `CardTile.test.tsx:41-47` call it unprovable in jsdom. But the hook returns it, and `renderHook` can call it with a stub node. Cover the four arms: `{ complete: true, naturalWidth: 300 }` gives `'shown'`, `naturalWidth: 0` gives `'failed'`, and `complete: false` and `null` stay `'loading'`. Then reword the comments [`ui/src/containers/useCardArt.ts:148-152`]
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **The Esc listener's `isComposing` and key arms are untested.** `CardDetail.test.tsx:513-531` checks only that a plain Escape releases the pin. Removing `event.isComposing`, or loosening the key test, would release the pin on an IME-cancelling Esc in the agent view's input, or on any key, and every test would pass. Add `{ key: 'Escape', isComposing: true }` and `{ key: 'Enter' }` cases that keep `pinnedId` [`ui/src/containers/CardDetail/CardDetail.tsx:380`]
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **The grid's `cost` and `typeLine` props reach no test.** They show only in a tile's named placeholder. `CardGrid.test.tsx` never fires `error`. Both props are strings, so swapping them, or dropping `typeLine=`, type-checks and passes. Render a grid card with both set, fire `error` on its `<img>`, and assert the placeholder's type line and pips [`ui/src/containers/CardGrid/CardGrid.tsx:136-137`]
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **The panel's cold-open image has no fetch priority.** About 99 tile images mount eagerly at once, with no `loading` or `fetchPriority`. On HTTP/1.1 to localhost the browser sends six at a time, so the panel's cold `large` key waits behind most of the grid. Set `fetchPriority="high"` on the panel's front image. The tile comment's rejection of `loading="lazy"` does not cover this [`ui/src/containers/CardDetail/CardDetail.tsx:492-510`]
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **Every deck refetch re-renders every tile.** `CardTile` is not memoised, and `CardGrid` maps a fresh `tiles` array on each new `boards` reference. Every agent-edit settle mints one, so all ~99 tiles re-run five hooks. Every prop is a primitive (`cardId`, `name`, `cost`, `typeLine`, `quantity`), so `React.memo` would limit a refetch to the rows that changed. This is the tile half of research F8, so fix it with that finding's store half [`ui/src/containers/CardTile/CardTile.tsx:226`, `ui/src/containers/CardGrid/CardGrid.tsx:126-139`]
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **Moving the target between two DFCs in different face states plays the flip rotation in the panel.** `.card-faces` is one element across targets. `data-flipped` goes `'true'` → `'false'` with the transform transition still armed (`.card-faces[data-flipped]`). So a hover from a flipped DFC to an unflipped one rotates the panel, while both `src`s have already changed to the new card. Key the faces element on `targetId`, so a target change remounts and only a flip animates [`ui/src/containers/CardDetail/CardDetail.tsx:488-491`]
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **The panel paints one frame without its art box on cold open.** `setDefaultTarget` runs in a passive effect, so the first commit has `targetId === null`. That frame shows the title with no art or text, and the next one pushes `DeckList` down by the art box. The header's "draws in full at first frame" (`:62`) holds only once a target exists. Reserve the art box while `targetId` is `null`, or correct the header [`ui/src/containers/CardDetail/CardDetail.tsx:351-358`, `:451`]
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **Line citations have drifted, and two cite the wrong thing.**
  - `CardTile.css:90` and `FlipControl.css:53` cite `AppShell.css:237` for `z-index: 20`; it is at `:274`.
  - `CardDetailChrome.css:170` and `CardTile.css:152` cite `Footer.css:107`; the rule is at `:108`.
  - `CardDetail.tsx:360` quotes `cards.ts:527`, but `cards.ts` has 325 lines.
  - `FlipControl.tsx:46` cites `CardTile.tsx:86-92` and `inspection.ts:43-54` as restatements of "a flip is not an inspection". `CardTile.tsx` has no such passage, and `inspection.ts:43-54` still says the control sits inside the tile's `<button>` and stops propagation to it, the shape this group abandoned.

  Cite symbols (F1's patch), and correct `inspection.ts`'s description.
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **One comment block states the `react-hooks/refs` rationale twice.** `CardDetail.tsx:263-265` ("DESTRUCTURED, and that is a lint requirement…") and `:272-273` ("BOTH DESTRUCTURED, for `react-hooks/refs`…") say the same thing. Delete one [`ui/src/containers/CardDetail/CardDetail.tsx:263-273`]
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **The second-change flash test cannot tell the two keyings apart, and its comment describes a defect that cannot occur.** With the effect keyed on `[flash.flashed]`, the first change's frame would fire on the same next frame that `[flash]`'s re-armed frame does. A second change can only land before that frame. So `CardTile.test.tsx:941-959` passes either way, and `CardTile.tsx:322-327` describes an early clear that never happens. Delete the test, or reword both to what keying on the object actually buys [`ui/src/containers/CardTile/CardTile.tsx:322-334`]

**Rejected**

- `false` — a single `requestAnimationFrame` can drop `data-flashed` before it paints. `setFlash` inside the frame callback is a default-lane update that React renders in a later task, after that frame's paint.
- `false` — `'{2}{G} // '` trims to `'{2}{G} //'` and skips the split (two findings, top-level and face-level). `cards.db` has 0 rows with an empty half, and no face-level cost carries `' // '`.
- `false` — a blank name leaves the tile's button unnamed. `CardTile` is mounted only by `CardGrid`, from the deck payload's `card.name`, and the corpus has 0 blank names.
- `false` — a non-zero face index on a non-flippable card shows the back face's text. `flipCard` is reachable only from a rendered `FlipControl` (2 or more imaged faces), and a hydrated entry never downgrades (`hydrateCard` returns it as is, and `seedDeckCards` writes `hydrated`).
- `low` — re-pinning straight to another printing with the same name is not re-announced. Real, but rare, and the fix adds a render or changes the copy.
- `low` — when the target changes while keyboard focus is on the panel's flip control or oracle scroller, the control unmounts and focus drops. It needs a mouse hover while focus sits in the panel, and the fix is focus management on unmount.
- `not a defect` — the blank-string helper copies (`given` ×2, `trimmedOrNull`). F3's patch already lists all three.
- `not a defect` — cold open, re-render volume and image throughput are decided outside F4's files (intent-alignment). Noted above for step 4.
- `not a defect` — the packet carries module source only, without tests, callers, the stores or the prior reviews (intent-alignment): all four were read during triage.

### 2026-11-08 — F5 UI containers, part 2: agent view shell, nav and suggestions

**Code review complete.** 3 `decision-needed`, 15 `patch`, 0 `defer`, 18 rejected.

- **Group:** F5. **Reviewed revision:** `ee0263d652a46f021515860bbc2a19e3dac60eed` (no change under `ui/src/containers/` up to `947524a`).
- **Files (11 files, 2,841 lines of non-test source):**
  - `AgentView/AgentView.css` (233), `AgentView.tsx` (545), `copy.ts` (48)
  - `AgentViewsNav/AgentViewsNav.css` (217), `AgentViewsNav.tsx` (619), `HistoryPopover.css` (159), `copy.ts` (115), `pushTime.ts` (56)
  - `SuggestionsView/SuggestionsView.css` (300), `SuggestionsView.tsx` (465), `copy.ts` (84)

  All paths are under `ui/src/containers/`. `focusHome.ts`, which F4 left to F5–F7 and which `AgentView` calls, is still unreviewed; it falls to F6 or F7. The three colocated suites (2,335 lines) were read as evidence only; T1 covers them. Read during triage: `ui/src/main.tsx`, `state/agentView.ts` (`openAgentView`, `reopenAgentView`, `reopenPush`, `suggestionsViewOf`), `state/cards.ts` (`hydrateCard`, `resetCardAttempts`), `CardDetail.tsx`'s Esc listener, `contracts.py`'s `_EventEnvelope`, `App.test.tsx:7120`, `eslint.config.js`, DESIGN.md and EXPERIENCE.md.
- **Mode:** no-code, thorough; lenses blind-hunter, edge-case-hunter, verification-gap, intent-alignment (none failed). Triage: leave as action items.
- **Overlap:**
  - The dead-gate decision below is the same as the F1, F3 and F4 decision, for this group's files. Rule on all four together.
  - The line-citation patch is F1's "cite symbols, not lines" patch (and F4's citation patch). Fix them together.
- **Outside this group's files (intent-alignment), for step 4:**
  - On a cold open only `AgentViewsNav` renders: five disabled pills. `AgentView` and `SuggestionsView` mount only while a view is open, so their cold-open cost is bundle and CSS weight. That weight is decided by `App.tsx`'s static imports (F8) and measured by `scripts/cdp_harness.py budget` (B13).
  - Whether the per-pill and per-row subscriptions re-render more than they should depends on `state/agentView.ts`'s selectors (F2) and `App.tsx`'s `useOpenAgentView` (F8). These files only consume them.
  - Thumbnail and record requests go through F4's `useCardArt`, `cardImageUrl` and `hydrateCard`'s dedupe.
  - Comparing the four agent-view bodies (per-field `typeof` gates, empty lines, the hydrate effect) crosses into F6–F7, where `SwapsView`, `TierListView` and `GroupsView` live.
- **What holds:**
  - **Request pattern:** `SuggestionsView` hydrates once per unique id, from an effect keyed on `items`. `hydrateCard` dedupes in-flight reads and terminal refusals, and the empty id is refused with no request. Images use the grid's own `normal` cache key, so a suggested card that joins the deck is already warm. The nav and the shell fetch nothing.
  - **Re-render scope:** each kind pill takes its own per-kind selectors, so a push re-renders its own pill. The History pill's store subscription exists only while the popover is open.
  - **Malformed pushes cost one row:** `itemOf` and the per-field gates degrade a bad element or field to the placeholder. The confidence enum is closed by a compile-time assert.
  - **Replace contract:** keying the heading's text fragment on `pushId` mutates the live region on every replace. The shell stays mounted, so the restore target survives a replace. A displaced kind is marked unread in `openAgentView`.
  - **Listener hygiene:** every document and node listener is registered once per open, through refs, and removed on cleanup. The Esc layering (view, then popover, then pin) holds whenever focus is inside the popover's wrapper. Esc from outside the wrapper closes the popover and the pin together, which is the accepted residual (EXPERIENCE.md, `App.test.tsx:7120`).

- [ ] [Review][Decision] **The focus trap does not keep focus inside the modal.** The trap is a `keydown` listener on the dialog, and nothing outside the dialog is `inert`. Focus reaches the background in two ways:
  - A mousedown on non-focusable dialog content blurs focus to `<body>`, because only scrim presses are `preventDefault`ed (`AgentView.tsx:442-451`). Examples are the kicker, the title, the count, header padding and the empty-push line. The next Shift+Tab then starts from the click point and walks out of the dialog, and the trap never sees it.
  - Focus entering from browser chrome (F6, or Tab from the address bar), or a screen reader's quick navigation, lands on the header pills in document order.

  So `AgentViewsNav.tsx:62-66`'s "Not reachable while a view is open" is false. The store handles a pill activated there safely: `openAgentView` replaces the view and marks the displaced kind unread. The History pill does not: `setOpen(true)` is hidden by `showPopover`, and the popover then springs open uninvited when the view closes. The subscription that resets `open` only fires on later `status: 'open'` writes. `keyboard-floor.test.ts`, which the comments cite as pinning the listener set, no longer exists. Options:
  - (a) Set `inert` on the shell's non-overlay content while a view is open. That is one attribute in `AppShell`, it is the platform primitive `aria-modal` assumes, it closes both routes, and it makes the nav's claim true.
  - (b) Add a document `focusin` guard that returns focus to the heading. This is the "fight the browser" listener the shell's header rejects.
  - (c) Keep the small trap, correct the nav's claim, and make the History pill's click a no-op while a view is open.

  Recommended: (a). It removes the escape routes and the History residue with one platform attribute instead of a listener. The shell's "could strand focus" worry does not apply to `inert`, which never moves focus.
- [ ] [Review][Decision] **A truncated reason cannot be read.** The reason is ellipsized to one line, and the stylesheet says the truncation is final on purpose (`SuggestionsView.css:276-296`). `contracts.py` allows 200 characters, which is longer than most rows are wide. `CardDetail` shows the card's fields, never the suggestion's `reason`. So a sighted user loses the end of the row's most important text with no way to recover it. Screen readers get the full text, since the ellipsis is visual only. Options:
  - (a) Put the full reason in the row's `title`. Pointer only, but one attribute.
  - (b) Let the reason wrap to two lines with `line-clamp: 2`. The row height and the thumbnail width then vary per row.
  - (c) Show the targeted suggestion's reason in the detail panel, which needs a new inspection-to-push link.
  - (d) Leave it, and tell agents to keep reasons short in the `companion_show_suggestions` docstring and the companion skill.

  Recommended: (d) plus (a). The tool docstring is where an agent learns the budget. `title` recovers the text for pointer users without changing the row layout the design fixed.
- [ ] [Review][Decision] **Comments cite deleted gates.** 37 citations in this group name 8 `ui/tests` files that no longer exist:
  - `token-usage.test.ts` (13), `keyboard-floor.test.ts` (8), `shell.test.ts` (8), `empty-push-copy.test.ts` (4)
  - `copy-rules.test.ts`, `agent-views-nav-copy.test.ts`, `wire-contract.test.ts` and `posture.test.ts` (1 each)

  Only `no-scryfall-hosts.test.ts` (1) survives. They describe as gated:
  - the listener census and phases that the Esc layering depends on (`AgentView.tsx:338`, `AgentViewsNav.tsx:402`);
  - the positive-`tabindex` ban the trap's arithmetic relies on (`AgentView.tsx:90`);
  - the reduced-motion pin behind the attribute-flip bloom (`AgentView.tsx:148`);
  - the `components/` set-equality guard (`AgentView.tsx:27`);
  - `SuggestionsView/copy.ts`'s import-free "verbatim gate";
  - the accent-dim assertion. `AgentView.css:30` homes it in `tests/token-usage.test.ts`, while `SuggestionsView.css:34` correctly points at `SuggestionsView.test.tsx`, whose source half is at `:760`.

  Options: the same as F4's. Recommended: the F1/F3/F4 ruling. Note that the listener-phase contract is the one most worth restoring as a real check, because the Esc layering of three components rests on it.
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **A `null` array element in a push is untested.** `itemOf` turns a non-object element into `{}`. But `SuggestionsView.test.tsx:440-462` feeds only objects with malformed fields, so dropping `itemOf` because the generated types say every element is a `PushedItem` would let one `null` element throw in render and unmount the dialog. Add a `SwapsView.test.tsx:310-317`-style case: `items={[null, ITEM]}` gives two rows, the first an unknown-card placeholder and the second still showing `ITEM.reason` [`ui/src/containers/SuggestionsView/SuggestionsView.tsx:112`, `:437`]
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **The first-paint `cardId === ''` guard is never observed.** `hydrateCard('')` writes the `unknown` entry synchronously inside the mount effect. So by the time any assertion runs, `isUnknownCard(entry)` already holds, and deleting `|| cardId === ''` would commit one `<img src="/api/card-image/">` and pass every test. Image requests bypass `fetch`, so `cardCalls()` cannot see it. Observe `document.body` with a `MutationObserver` set up before `render`, and assert that no `IMG` was ever added for an item with no id [`ui/src/containers/SuggestionsView/SuggestionsView.tsx:251`]
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **A press on the History pill while the popover is open is untested.** The outside-pointerdown check treats the whole wrapper as inside, so pressing the pill falls through to its toggle. The only test presses an entry, which is inside both the wrapper and the popover root. Narrowing the check to the popover root would make a real click close on `pointerdown` and reopen on `click`, and every test would pass. Fire `pointerDown` and then `click` on `historyPill()`, and assert the popover is gone [`ui/src/containers/AgentViewsNav/AgentViewsNav.tsx:419-425`]
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **`closePopover`'s focus-on-`<body>` branch is untested.** Every dismissal test starts with focus on an entry, on the pill or on a live outside control. `<body>` is reachable: a press on the popover's padding is inside the wrapper, so it does not dismiss, but it blurs focus. Reducing the condition to `inWrapper` would leave focus on `<body>` after Esc. If a push then arrives, the view would record no restore target. Blur the active element, fire Esc at `document.body`, and assert focus lands on `historyPill()` [`ui/src/containers/AgentViewsNav/AgentViewsNav.tsx:346`]
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **The push-replace crossfade does not play.** `.agent-view-title` and `.agent-view-body` carry `transition: opacity` on their base rules, and `[data-replacing='true']` does not turn it off. So `setReplacing(true)` starts a 240 ms fade towards 0, and the frame callback flips it back about one frame later, which shows as a dip of a few percent. The entry bloom avoids this only because its starting state exists at mount. Make the replace start from opacity 0 with no transition, then fade in over `--motion-glide`. Check in a real browser, since jsdom evaluates no CSS [`ui/src/containers/AgentView/AgentView.css:133`, `:204`, `:230-233`; `AgentView.tsx:323-325`]
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **Under StrictMode, a closed view returns focus to the wrong place.** `main.tsx` mounts the app in `<StrictMode>`, so in dev the mount effect runs, cleans up and runs again. The second run captures `document.activeElement`, which is by then the view's own heading. On close the heading is detached, so arm 3 sends focus to the state panel or the `<h1>` instead of the opener. Production is unaffected, but every manual check in `npm run dev` sees the wrong return. Do not overwrite the restore target with an element inside `dialogRef` [`ui/src/containers/AgentView/AgentView.tsx:208-214`]

**Rejected**

- `false` — `setReplacing(true)` in the effect body contradicts the lint claim. `recommended-latest` (`eslint-plugin-react-hooks` 7.1.1) passes on master, so the rule does not flag it, and the only real effect is the crossfade timing (patch above).
- `false` — `focusablesIn` ignores hidden ancestors (two lenses). No agent view nests a control inside a `[hidden]`, `aria-hidden` or `fieldset` ancestor; the `aria-hidden` spans are decorative leaves.
- `false` — the restore target can be disabled or hidden while connected. `ui/src` has no non-test `disabled={…}`, kind pills never go active to quiet, and a removed opener takes arm 3.
- `false` — an id-less envelope makes two pushes compare equal. `_EventEnvelope.id` is required and non-blank (`contracts.py:961`), so the backend never broadcasts one.
- `false` — the popover's document-level Esc fires after CardDetail's mount-registered listener and releases the pin too. That is the accepted residual (EXPERIENCE.md), and `App.test.tsx:7120` pins it.
- `false` — the thumbnail's `auto` column collapses to zero width. Grid sizing re-resolves `auto` columns once row heights are known (CSS Grid §12.1 step 3), and the row shipped through c6-7 with visible thumbnails (`00f401f` reasons about the width following the row height).
- `low` — keying rows by `id:index` remounts the later rows when a replace prepends an item. A replace already crossfades the body and moves focus to the heading, and the fix adds occurrence counting.
- `low` — sighted keyboard users never see the quiet pill's explanation. `disabled` plus a description is DESIGN.md:684's ruling, and the sentence only restates what the dimmed pill shows.
- `low` — `EMPTY_PUSH_NOUNS: Record<string, string>` loses exhaustiveness, and a prototype key could resolve. `agentEventOf` admits only known kinds, `agentView.test.ts` checks that the tables agree, and the import-free rule behind the loose type is part of the dead-gate ruling.
- `low` — in Safari, an outside press on a kind pill records the History pill as the view's return target (Safari does not focus a clicked button). Safari only, focus lands one pill over, and the fix is not direct.
- `low` — a retryable hydration failure is not retried while the view stays open. Hovering the row re-hydrates it through the panel, `resetCardAttempts` re-arms on reconnect, and a retry would need a connection subscription.
- `low` — every row subscribes to `usePinnedId`, so a pin change re-renders every row. There are at most the contract's item cap of rows, the cost is invisible, and the fix adds a selector.
- `not a defect` — re-render and request volume, cold-open cost, bundle weight and test design live in the store, `App`, the harness and the suites (intent-alignment, five points). They are noted above for step 4 and T1.

  - None of the 14 files fetches anything. `FormatCheck` reads `useFormatCheck()`, and its one request per settled deck belongs to `App.tsx` (F8) and `state/formatCheck.ts` (F1). Every other panel reads `boards` and the card cache. So request volume and cold-open cost are decided in the store, `App` and the harness.
  - The architecture report's F8 (`App` subscribes with no selector, so every `App` render re-renders all four panels) lands here only at the receiving end. None of the four panels is memoised, and the curve and colour derivations run in render.
  - `ColourDistribution` adds a second re-render driver of its own: it subscribes to the whole card map, so it re-renders on every cache write. That means two seeds per cold open, plus every `hydrateCard` for an id outside the deck (agent-view rows, or a detail-panel target outside the deck). Each render is cheap (about 48 µs per `coloursOf` call over 99 rows, as measured), but bounding how many there are is the store's job.
  - The only bundle weight in this group is the four stylesheets.
- **What holds:**
  - **No second derivation:** `DeckList`, `curveOf` and `coloursOf` all walk `boardsOf`'s partition, and none of them filters, sorts or regroups cards. The sideboard is excluded by not reading it. Every count is a summed quantity, never a row count.
  - **Hooks discipline:** `DeckRow` subscribes per row (`useIsLiveTarget`, `useCardEntry`). `ColourDistribution`'s single whole-map subscription returns the stored reference, so it cannot loop.
  - **Closed vocabularies:** a compile-time assert holds every copy map to its union in both directions (`TypeGroup`, `ManaColour`, the check names and the statuses). `TONE_FOR_STATUS` is held to `BADGE_TONES`, so a mistyped tone cannot reach `Badge`.
  - **Format check:** it renders rows in the payload's `CHECK_ORDER`, binds nothing to `is_legal`, and adds no Tab stop. The empty-deck hide is at `App.tsx:213`.
  - **Empty states:** the curve hides when its total is zero, and the colour bar hides when its pip total is zero. Both divisions are guarded (`total === 0`, and segments are filtered before the percentage is taken).

- [ ] [Review][Decision] **No control exposes the pin state.** Clicking a deck row toggles the pin (`DeckList.tsx:199`), but the row carries no `aria-pressed`. The live state is only a class (`is-live`) with a tint, so a screen-reader user on a row cannot tell whether pressing it will pin or unpin. Tiles (F4) and agent-view thumbnails (F5) work the same way. In EXPERIENCE.md's model, a pin is announced once through `CardDetail`'s polite region, and the detail panel shows its unpin control only while a card is pinned. Options:
  - (a) Add `aria-pressed` to rows, tiles and thumbnails, read from a per-id pinned selector. Every one of these controls then reads as a toggle button, and on a large deck about 200 of them say "not pressed".
  - (b) Keep the announcement model and write it into EXPERIENCE.md: the pin state is announced when it changes and exposed by the detail panel's unpin control, not on each control.

  Recommended: (b). The unpin control already exposes the state in one place, and 200 "not pressed" announcements cost more than they tell the user.

- `false` — a `NaN` or missing `cmc` drops a card from the curve. `cards.cmc` is a non-null `Float`, `CardSummary.cmc` is a required `float`, and JSON cannot carry `NaN`.
- `false` — the copy maps are mutable, so an importer could rewrite on-screen copy. No code writes to them, and their keys are already held to the unions.
- `low` — in the format check, "not loaded yet" and "refused" both render nothing. That is the documented behaviour of `state/formatCheck.ts` (F1's file), and showing a refusal would be new UI.
- `not a defect` — request volume, cold-open cost, `App`'s selector-less subscription and the cross-surface statistics all live outside this group's files (intent-alignment). They are noted above for step 4.

  - `ConnectionPill/ConnectionPill.css` (237), `ConnectionPill.tsx` (250), `copy.ts` (140), `port.ts` (34)
  - `DeckAnnouncer/DeckAnnouncer.tsx` (144), `copy.ts` (53)
  - `SkipLink/SkipLink.css` (130), `SkipLink.tsx` (148), `copy.ts` (33)
  - `focusHome.ts` (116)

  All paths are under `ui/src/containers/`. This group covers `focusHome.ts`, which F4–F6 left unreviewed, so F4–F7 have now reviewed every non-test file under `ui/src/containers/`. The six colocated suites were read as evidence only; T1 covers them. Read during triage: `App.tsx` (the surface-transition rescue and the static imports), `AgentView.tsx`'s `focusHome` calls, `CardTile.tsx`'s badge naming, `components/AppShell/AppShell.css`, `styles/visually-hidden.css`, `ConnectionPill.test.tsx`'s announcement cases, `contracts.py`'s `SwapItem`, `deck_management.py`'s blank-name check, DESIGN.md's `group-section` block, EXPERIENCE.md's state-pattern rows, UX-DR45 (`archive-v6/epics-companion-app.md`), and commit `1305de4` with the deferred-work entry it added.
- **Mode:** no-code, thorough; lenses blind-hunter, edge-case-hunter, verification-gap, intent-alignment (none failed). Triage: leave as action items.
- **Overlap:**
  - The dead-gate decision below is the same as the F1, F3, F4, F5 and F6 decision, for this group's files. Rule on all six together.
  - The citation patch is F1's "cite symbols, not lines" patch (and F4's, F5's and F6's). Fix them together.
  - The live-region count patch is F5's "three comments disagree on how many live regions the app has", in two more files. Fix them together.
  - The tile-duplication decision is the comparison F5 handed on ("the four agent-view bodies … crosses into F6–F7"). `SuggestionRow` (F5) is the fourth copy.
  - The pin-state decision F6 raised (rows, tiles and thumbnails carry no `aria-pressed`) also covers this group's `SwapTile`, `TierTile` and `GroupTile`, so it is not repeated here.
  - The group-tile width decision was already recorded on 2026-08-23, as a deferred-work entry from `spec-tier-list-usability` (now on the `process` branch) and as a follow-up note in DESIGN.md's `group-section` block. It is raised again because it is still open on master.
- **Outside this group's files (intent-alignment), for step 4:**
  - On a cold open, only `ConnectionPill`, `DeckAnnouncer` and `SkipLink` from this group render, and none of them fetches anything. The three view bodies mount only while a view is open. `App.tsx` imports all six statically, so their bundle and CSS weight is decided there (F8) and measured by `scripts/cdp_harness.py budget` (B13).
  - Whether the per-tile subscriptions re-render more than they should depends on the selectors in `state/` (F1, F2). These are `useCardEntry`, `useFaceIndex`, `useCardArt` and `usePinnedId`, plus `useDeckCardQuantity` in groups. Each hydration effect is keyed on `items`, so its cost also depends on whether `items` keeps its identity across store writes, which `state/agentView.ts` (F2) decides.
  - The architecture rules the intent names (AD-2, AD-3, the Python layering, stateless tools, AD-15) have no surface in these files. Only the UI decisions (AD-7, AD-11, AD-12) do, and those hold (below).
- **What holds:**
  - **Request pattern:** each view hydrates once per unique id, from an effect keyed on `items`. A `Set` collapses duplicates across rows and sides. Blank and non-string ids cost no request: groups and tiers filter them out, and swaps fold them to `''`. `hydrateCard` dedupes in-flight and terminal ids, so `TierPreview`'s own hydration costs nothing for an id a tile already holds. Every image goes through `cardImageUrl` with the rendition unspelled, so tiles, the preview and the grid share one browser-cache key (AD-11).
  - **Re-render scope:** `ConnectionPill` and `DeckAnnouncer` subscribe through primitive selectors (the deck name, the two counts, the deck id, the open-view boolean and the settle counter), so a board change does not re-render them. An instance-id change does not announce.
  - **Malformed pushes cost one row or one tile:** every item field is read as `unknown` and gated. The tier letters and the swap confidences are closed vocabularies held by compile-time asserts. A malformed tier or group is skipped, and its neighbours still render.
  - **`DeckAnnouncer`:** a settle that lands behind an open view is consumed, not queued. The keyed fragment re-announces an unchanged total. The sentence is cleared when its deck departs.
  - **`focusHome`:** it refuses non-elements, leaves an authored `tabindex` alone, and removes its own `tabindex` when focus is refused.

- [ ] [Review][Decision] **Group tiles have no width, so they collapse to slivers.** `.group-tile-thumb` is a flex box with `min-height: 6lh`, and its children are either absolutely positioned or empty. `card-shape`'s `aspect-ratio` cannot derive a width from an auto-height box with no in-flow content. This is the mechanism that collapsed the tier strip to "overlapping ~0px slivers" until `1305de4` gave `.tier-tile-thumb` DESIGN.md's `thumb-width: 176px`. `GroupsView.css:164-170` calls the same width "the outstanding fix", and DESIGN.md's `group-section` block records it as a follow-up. `.swap-tile-thumb` has the same rule. Its tile is a flex column whose "OUT · N COPIES" label sets the width, so it renders correctly only by coincidence (`SwapsView.css:126-133`). Nobody has checked the groups view in a real browser since. Options:
  - (a) Give `.group-tile-thumb` and `.swap-tile-thumb` the tier strip's `176px`, and record it in DESIGN.md's `group-section` and `swap-row` blocks.
  - (b) Rule a smaller group or swap thumbnail width in DESIGN.md first, since a wrapping strip of 176px tiles takes more room than a tier's scrolling one, then apply it.
  - (c) Fix groups alone now, and size the swap tile only if its label ever stops setting the width.

  Recommended: (a). It is the fix already proved on the tier strip, it needs one DESIGN.md line per block, and it removes the coincidence the swap tile depends on. Check it in a real browser, since jsdom resolves no layout. [`ui/src/containers/GroupsView/GroupsView.css:176-181`, `ui/src/containers/SwapsView/SwapsView.css:137-142`]
- [ ] [Review][Decision] **In the groups and swaps views, hover and focus target a panel nobody can see.** `TierPreview` exists because "the persistent `CardDetail` panel sits BEHIND the agent view's scrim, so inside this view the target was invisible" (`TierListView.tsx:261-264`). `GroupTile` and `SwapTile` set the same inspection target but have no preview. So a hover or focus in those views shows nothing, and a click pins a card whose detail stays hidden until the view closes. Options:
  - (a) Lift `TierPreview` into a shared in-view preview and mount it in the groups and swaps bodies too, with a DESIGN.md `components.*-preview` line for each layout.
  - (b) Leave groups and swaps without a preview, and write in EXPERIENCE.md that only the tier list previews in place.

  Recommended: (a). The tier-list spec already ruled that an invisible target makes the view unusable, and the swap and group tiles follow the same inspection contract. The preview is self-contained (it reads `useInspectionTargetId` and writes nothing), so it can move without new state. [`ui/src/containers/GroupsView/GroupsView.tsx:204-216`, `ui/src/containers/SwapsView/SwapsView.tsx:189-201`, `ui/src/containers/TierListView/TierListView.tsx:282`]
- [ ] [Review][Decision] **The four near-copies of the agent-view tile have already drifted apart.** `SwapTile`, `TierTile`, `GroupTile` and F5's `SuggestionRow` each re-declare the same parts:
  - `itemOf`, `renderableOf` and `isUnknownCard`;
  - the five-verb button;
  - the unknown-target release effect;
  - the unknown → named → loading placeholder ladder.

  Groups and tiers also share `cardIdsOf` verbatim. The comments call these copies "verbatim", but they now differ:
  - the width fix landed in one stylesheet of the three (decision above);
  - the `cardId === ''` guard is live in swaps, dead in tiers and removed in groups (patch below);
  - titles are trimmed in groups but not in tiers (patch below).

  Every later fix has to find each copy by hand. Options:
  - (a) Extract a shared tile (button, verbs, release effect, ladder) with slots for the label and the badge, and a shared module for the untrusted-item helpers.
  - (b) Extract only the helpers, and the release effect as a hook, and keep the JSX in each view.
  - (c) Keep the copies, and make each header name its siblings so a fix reaches all of them.

  Recommended: (b). It removes the copies whose behaviour has drifted (the gates and the release valve) without folding three different tile layouts into one component with slots. [`ui/src/containers/TierListView/TierListView.tsx:86`, `:146-161`, `:195-200`; `ui/src/containers/SwapsView/SwapsView.tsx:80`, `:128-135`, `:175-180`; `ui/src/containers/GroupsView/GroupsView.tsx:92`, `:136-151`, `:196-201`]
- [ ] [Review][Decision] **The pill's announcement policy leaves two cases unruled.** The announcement is keyed on the connection status and nothing else (`ConnectionPill.tsx:164-171`):
  - **Behind an open agent view:** UX-DR45 lists the pill as a live region, and EXPERIENCE.md's deck-refetch row rules that "no announcement fires from behind a modal". `DeckAnnouncer` follows that rule by gating on `useAgentViewIsOpen()`. The pill has no such gate, so a reconnect or a disconnect speaks while a view is open. Neither file says whether connection status is exempt.
  - **The first connect on every cold load:** the `seen: null` sentinel silences only the first render, whose status is `'reconnecting'`. The first `reconnecting → live` change then announces "Connected" on every page load, usually before the deck name has arrived. The header's own reason for the sentinel (not to announce "before it has failed at anything") argues against this too. `ConnectionPill.test.tsx:512-517` pins it as an ordinary transition.

  Options:
  - (a) Add `DeckAnnouncer`'s modal gate, and treat the first `reconnecting → live` as part of the mount silence.
  - (b) Keep speaking behind the modal and write that exemption into EXPERIENCE.md (a lost connection matters inside a dialog too), and silence the first connect.
  - (c) Keep both behaviours, and write both into EXPERIENCE.md.

  Recommended: (b). Losing the backend is news wherever focus is, while "Connected" on every page load is noise. Silencing the first connect is one more term in the capture's condition. [`ui/src/containers/ConnectionPill/ConnectionPill.tsx:155-171`]
- [ ] [Review][Decision] **A one-copy swap reads "1 copies".** The label is always `` `${word} · ${qty} copies` ``, following `contracts.py`'s `SwapItem` docstring ("render as the literal "Out · N copies""). In singleton formats nearly every swap is one-for-one, so the common case shows "OUT · 1 COPIES". `DeckAnnouncer/copy.ts` does give "1 card" a singular, citing `ManaCurve/copy.ts`'s precedent, so the app has no single plural rule. Options:
  - (a) Render "1 copy" for exactly one, and amend the `SwapItem` docstring. The docstring is wire-visible, so regenerate the OpenAPI types too.
  - (b) Keep the literal, and record in `SwapsView.tsx` why the swap label does not follow the announcer's precedent.

  Recommended: (a). It fixes the grammar of the common case, and zero still reads "0 copies" either way. [`ui/src/containers/SwapsView/SwapsView.tsx:182-186`]
- [ ] [Review][Decision] **Comments cite deleted gates.** 32 citations in this group name 10 `ui/tests` files that commit `e584980` removed:
  - `token-usage.test.ts` (9), `shell.test.ts` (5), `keyboard-floor.test.ts` (4)
  - `wire-contract.test.ts` (3), `empty-push-copy.test.ts` (3)
  - `deck-announcement-copy.test.ts`, `copy-rules.test.ts` and `connection-pill-copy.test.ts` (2 each)
  - `pin-announcement-copy.test.ts` and `copy-tails.test.ts` (1 each)

  The comments describe these as gated:

  - the one shared empty-push sentence (the `kind` prop docs in all three views);
  - the pill's and the announcer's copy. The "NO IMPORTS" `copy.ts` headers exist so that `ui/tests` can import them, and no `ui/tests` file does now;
  - `ConnectionPill/copy.ts`'s `RETRIES_QUIETLY` mirror, which "fails `copy-tails.test.ts`". `components/StatePanel/states.test.ts:110` still pins the coupling;
  - the full-window fixed-layer guard that `SkipLink.css` reasons against.

  Options: the same as F4's. Recommended: the F1/F3/F4/F5/F6 ruling. In this group, the `wire-contract` alias ban is the one most worth restoring as a real check, because three files name their types around it.
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **A swap tile's accessible name leaves out the card.** The only text in the `<button>` is the label, and the image is `alt=""`. So a screen reader reads every tile as "Out · 2 copies" or "In · 2 copies", and the user cannot tell which cards are traded. The card detail that would say it sits behind the scrim. `TierTile` and `GroupTile` add a visually hidden `frontFaceName` span for exactly this reason. Add the same span after the label, and correct the header's claim that the label is "what a screen reader calls the control". `SwapsView.test.tsx:357` queries by the label alone and will need the name added [`ui/src/containers/SwapsView/SwapsView.tsx:141-146`, `:203`]
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **No tile test covers a failed image.** None of the three view suites fires `error` on a tile `<img>`. `TierListView.test.tsx:594-606` fails only the preview's image, and `App.test.tsx:6132-6336` never fails one. If the `art === 'failed'` arm were dropped from any tile, a failed image would leave that tile on the loading well, and every test would pass. In each suite, fire `error` on one tile image and assert three things: the `<img>` is gone, the named placeholder shows the cached name, and the tile is still a button (the arrangement of `SuggestionsView.test.tsx:350`) [`ui/src/containers/GroupsView/GroupsView.tsx:230`, `ui/src/containers/SwapsView/SwapsView.tsx:211`, `ui/src/containers/TierListView/TierListView.tsx:229`]
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **No tile test covers a flipped face.** Every tile `src` assertion runs after `resetFaces()`, so at face 0. The only test that seeds the faces store, `TierListView.test.tsx:578-592`, asserts on the preview. If any tile passed `undefined` for `face`, it would show the front face while the grid shows the back, and every test would pass. In each suite, seed `faces: { '<id>': 1 }` and assert that the tile's `src` ends in `?face=1` [`ui/src/containers/GroupsView/GroupsView.tsx:246`, `ui/src/containers/SwapsView/SwapsView.tsx:227`, `ui/src/containers/TierListView/TierListView.tsx:245`]
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **No test asserts the preview's own hydration request.** The tileless-pin test (`TierListView.test.tsx:622-639`) forces the outcome with `seedUnknown`, and every `cardCalls()` test renders with no pin. If `TierPreview`'s hydration effect were deleted, a pin carried over from another surface would show no name, cost or type, and a dead pin would never be released. Every test would still pass. With an empty cache and a pin outside the push, assert that `cardCalls()` contains `/api/cards/<id>` before seeding the settle [`ui/src/containers/TierListView/TierListView.tsx:298-300`]
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **`TierListView` still describes the blank-id path that `cardIdsOf` removed.** `cardIdsOf` now filters out blank and non-string ids before any tile mounts. So:

  - The hydration effect's "after a malformed tier heals (it cannot; but …)" contradicts itself. `GroupsView` has the corrected wording.

- [ ] [Review][Patch] **Tier names and notes are not trimmed, but group titles and rationales are.** `GroupsView.titleOf` trims so that "'  Ramp  ' renders as 'Ramp'", to match the store's trim of the payload-level title. `TierListView.nameOf` and `noteOf` blank-check the trimmed value but return the raw one, and the raw name also goes into the row's key. The overlap also rests on a hand-estimated "~31px" pill height. Keep the bridge for the pointer, but move the overlap clear of the ring (for example, extend the bridge by the ring's width plus its offset). Check it in a real browser [`ui/src/containers/ConnectionPill/ConnectionPill.css:186-189`, `:211`]

But the JSX renders `{deckName}` inside the `named === null` branch. The two values agree today. Render `named`, so the claim holds by construction [`ui/src/containers/ConnectionPill/ConnectionPill.tsx:143-145`, `:222`]

Today's label is short enough to hide the miss. Undo it as well, or narrow the claim [`ui/src/containers/SkipLink/SkipLink.css:49-52`]

After a deck switch or rename, the region still holds the old deck's name, readable in browse mode. That is by design, since a name change alone must not announce. But `DeckAnnouncer.tsx:244-245` cites the pill as "a current-state reading that never goes stale". Correct the comparison [`ui/src/containers/DeckAnnouncer/DeckAnnouncer.tsx:244-245`, `ui/src/containers/ConnectionPill/ConnectionPill.tsx:160-162`]
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **Group titles are not headings.** DESIGN.md sets the title in `{typography.heading}`, but it renders as a `<span>`, so a screen-reader user cannot move between groups by heading. The view's own title is the dialog's `h2`. Render the group title as an `h3`, with the count still beside it [`ui/src/containers/GroupsView/GroupsView.tsx:292-295`]

**Rejected**

- `false` — the tier letter is `aria-hidden`, so a screen reader never hears the rank. This is deliberate. The wire refuses a blank `name` because the name is "the accessible carrier of rank" (`contracts.py`), and the chip renders the name as text.
- `false` — `TierPreview` keeps fetching images while it is hidden below 1100px. Its `src` is the same URL as the tile thumbnail it previews, so the request is a warm browser-cache hit. Only a pin from another surface could miss the cache, and that costs one image.
- `false` — an empty deck name renders "Connected — ". `create_deck` and `update_deck` refuse a blank name (`deck_management.py:344`).
- `low` — a flapping socket announces every flip. The socket loop's exponential backoff limits how often that happens, and coalescing the announcements would need timers.
- `low` — the fixed pill may cover the bottom of the left column at the end of a scroll (WCAG 2.4.11). This is unverified: it depends on the column's padding at the scroll end, and settling it needs a check in a real browser. If it is real, the pill covers only part of the content, which 2.4.11 allows.
- `low` — `GroupTile`'s "×N" joins its accessible name without saying "in this deck". `CardTile` names its badge under the same recorded rule, and a phrase giving the meaning would be new copy.
- `low` — a refused `focusHome` leaves its `once` blur listener attached. There is at most one per refusal, it removes the same attribute that a later call sets, and no heading carries an authored `tabindex`.
- `not a defect` — the cold-open budget, bundle weight, selector granularity and test design are decided in `App.tsx`, `state/`, the harness and the suites (intent-alignment). They are noted above for step 4 and T1.

### 2026-11-09 — F8 UI shell and styles

**Code review complete.** 4 `decision-needed`, 6 `patch`, 0 `defer`, 0 rejected.

- **Group:** F8, the last group of step 2. **Reviewed revision:** `ee0263d652a46f021515860bbc2a19e3dac60eed` (no change to these files up to `01299e7`).
- **Files (9 files, 1,210 lines):** `ui/src/App.tsx` (270), `ui/src/main.tsx` (10), `ui/src/index.css` (82), and under `ui/src/styles/`: `tokens.css` (525), `surfaces.ts` (76), `surfaces.test.ts` (79), `card-geometry.css` (66), `fonts.css` (59), `visually-hidden.css` (43). Read during triage: `state/formatCheck.ts` (`clearFormatCheck`, `loadFormatCheck`), `FormatCheck.tsx`'s one branch, `state/systemState.ts`'s `useSystemState`, EXPERIENCE.md's format-check and deck-refetch rows, what is left of `ui/tests/`, `ui/tests/lint-gates.test.ts`'s iteration-count case, `.stylelintrc.json`, and every `visually-hidden`, `pointer-events` and focus-ring use under `ui/src/`.
- **Mode:** no-code, quick; one lens (none failed). Triage: leave as action items.
- **Overlap:**
  - The `App` subscription patch is the root half of the architecture report's F8. F2's `seedDeckCards` patch and F4's `CardTile` memo patch are the other halves. Fix the three together.
  - The dead-gate decision is the F1, F3, F4, F5, F6 and F7 decision, for this group's files. Rule on all seven together. The reduced-motion and offline-asset decisions are split out of it because each guards a user-facing requirement, not just a comment.
  - The citation patch is F1's "cite symbols, not lines" patch (and F4–F7's). Fix them together.
  - The focus-ring decision overlaps F6's option (b), forced colours as an app-wide policy. Both decide what `index.css` owns globally.
- **Handed on from earlier groups, answered here:**
  - **Bundle weight (F5, F7):** `App.tsx` imports all four agent-view bodies statically, so the build is one JS chunk (about 250 KB, uncompressed) and one stylesheet (about 36 KB). Lazy-loading the four bodies would take them off the cold-open path. Whether that is worth it on a localhost load is a step 4 call, measured with `scripts/cdp_harness.py budget` (B13). Not raised as a finding.
  - **Format-check trigger (F1, F6):** one `loadFormatCheck` per settled `detail`, with a generation guard against stale writes. The request count holds, but the clear between requests removes the panel (patch below).
- **What holds:**
  - `useSystemState`, `useDeckState` and `useAgentConnection` are each mounted once, in `App`, as their headers require. The surface precedence is read from `surfaceOf`, never re-derived.
  - The empty-deck and booting arms make no request and draw no image, so a cold open's first frame is empty rather than the Welcome surface.
  - `index.css`'s box-sizing reset and `body` margin are the only global element rules. No stylesheet outside `tokens.css` declares a custom property today (unenforced; decision below).
  - Every motion override in `tokens.css` matches its component selector today (`.card-tile-frame:hover`, the flip classes, `.agent-view[data-entering]`, `.app-shell-identity[data-updating]`, `.card-tile-quantity[data-flashed]`).
  - `fonts.css` loads only the bundled Space Grotesk file. The one `https` string in these files is a licence URL inside a comment.

- [ ] [Review][Decision] **The reduced-motion overrides depend on selector text in other files, and nothing checks it now.** `tokens.css` neutralises motion for `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` by repeating six component selectors with `!important`. Its comments say a guard "compares selector TEXT" and turns the suite red on a mismatch, but that guard was `tests/token-usage.test.ts`, deleted in `e584980`. `tests/tokens.test.ts` checks the motion token values only. So renaming `.card-tile-frame`, `.agent-view` or the flip classes would silently bring back the 1.06 scale, the 8px rise or the 3D flip for reduced-motion users. jsdom does not evaluate media queries, so no component test can notice. The cross-file design is also why `!important` is needed: `tokens.css` loses on source order. Options:
  - (a) Move each `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)` override into the component stylesheet beside the rule it cancels. Same specificity plus later source order wins without `!important`, and the token layer stops naming component classes.
  - (b) Keep the overrides in `tokens.css`, and check them in a real browser with emulated reduced motion through the CDP harness.
  - (c) Keep them, and reword the comments to say the match is held by review only.

  Recommended: (a). It removes the coupling instead of guarding it, and it drops the `!important`s. Check each moved override once in a real browser with reduced motion emulated. [`ui/src/styles/tokens.css:373-397`, `:416-424`, `:475-477`, `:497-503`, `:522-524`]
- [ ] [Review][Decision] **The offline guarantee for fonts and assets is no longer enforced, but the comments say it is.** `fonts.css` says `tests/fonts.test.ts` "fails the build on any external reference anywhere in the committed bundle", and `tokens.css` relies on "the bundle URL scan". `fonts.test.ts` is gone. `no-scryfall-hosts.test.ts` bans only Scryfall hosts, and `.stylelintrc.json` has no URL rule. So a Google Fonts `@import url('https://…')` or an `src: url('https://…')` passes every gate, which breaks NFR-06 and UX-DR2. Options:
  - (a) Add stylelint's `function-url-scheme-allowed-list` (no `http` or `https`) and correct both comments.
  - (b) Widen `no-scryfall-hosts.test.ts` into a scan of the built bundle for any external URL, and correct both comments.
  - (c) Reword both comments to say the guarantee is held by review only.

  Recommended: (a). It is one config line, it fails at the source rather than after a build, and it covers every stylesheet. [`ui/src/styles/fonts.css:13-15`, `ui/src/styles/tokens.css:62-64`]
- [ ] [Review][Decision] **The rest of this group's comments cite deleted gates.** The citations:
  - `App.tsx:24-26` cites `keyboard-floor.test.ts` for "one module writes `.tabIndex`". It holds today (`focusHome.ts` only).
  - `App.tsx:81-82` and `visually-hidden.css:10-12` cite `shell.test.ts` and `posture.test.ts` for "a container may not reach the network". `eslint.config.js` has no import restriction that replaces them.
  - `tokens.css:25-27`: no other stylesheet may declare a custom property. It holds today.
  - `tokens.css:60`, `:134-135` cite `findRoleWithoutCompanions` and `findUnpairedNumericRole`. Every type-role block has its companions today.
  - `tokens.css:100-102`, `:205-207`, `:214-215`, `:237-238`, `:262-263`, `:284` and `:467-468` cite `token-usage.test.ts`.
  - `card-geometry.css:37-39`: the `CARD_SHAPED` allowlist "is a GATE".
  - `surfaces.ts:14-15`, `:22-24` (see the patch below).
  - One rule is now held by nothing: `tokens.css:331-334` says nothing loops under any setting, and `lint-gates.test.ts:543-548` shows that stylelint misses a numeric iteration count in the `animation` shorthand. Only the deleted `token-usage.test.ts` caught it.

  Options: the same as F1's and F3's. Recommended: the F1/F3/F4/F5/F6/F7 ruling. If it is (b), restore real checks for the cheap ones: a stylelint rule banning custom-property declarations outside `tokens.css`, a `declaration-property-value-disallowed-list` entry banning `var(--radius-card)` outside `card-geometry.css`, and a ban on numeric iteration counts. `lint-gates.test.ts:546-548` also cites the deleted file (T1).
- [ ] [Review][Decision] **The focus ring is written out 15 times, and `index.css` has no default.** The same `outline: var(--focus-ring-width) solid var(--focus-ring)` appears in 14 stylesheets (`CardDetailChrome.css` twice), almost always with `outline-offset: var(--focus-ring-offset)`. DESIGN.md requires a visible `{components.focus-ring}` on every interactive element, so a new control without its own rule ships without a ring. `index.css` already owns global rules for the same reason (box-sizing). Options:
  - (a) Add one low-specificity `:where(:focus-visible)` rule to `index.css`, and keep only the variants (the tile's offset-0 ring, the ring over art).
  - (b) Keep the per-component rules, and add a stylelint or test check that every interactive stylesheet declares one.
  - (c) Keep them as they are.

  Recommended: (a). It makes the requirement hold by default and deletes about a dozen copies. Decide it together with F6's forced-colours option (b), since both set what `index.css` owns. Check the rings in a real browser afterwards. [`ui/src/index.css`, `ui/src/components/Footer/Footer.css`, `ui/src/containers/CardDetail/CardDetailChrome.css`, and 12 more]
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **Every agent edit removes the format-check panel until the new report arrives.** The effect is keyed on `detail`, which changes on every settled refetch. On each one the cleanup runs `clearFormatCheck()`, which resets the slice to `INITIAL_FORMAT_CHECK_STATE`. `loadFormatCheck` then writes `{status: 'loading'}`, and `FormatCheck` draws nothing unless the status is `'report'`. So after every `deck_changed` the panel at the bottom of the right column disappears for one round trip and comes back, and the column reflows. This breaks EXPERIENCE.md's "Right column, always present" (format-check row), "never a blank or skeleton teardown of a populated view" (deck-refetch row) and "Blank screens are never shown after first paint". `App.test.tsx:3900` and `:3937` count requests only, so nothing checks that the panel survives a refetch. For a refetch of the same deck id, keep the old report on screen until the new one lands (bump the generation, do not reset the state). Still clear when the id changes, the deck empties or the deck leaves the screen. Add a test that the panel stays rendered across a refetch [`ui/src/App.tsx:100-107`, `ui/src/state/formatCheck.ts:154-157`, `:201`]
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **`App` re-renders on system-store writes it never reads.** `useSystemState()` returns `useSystemStore()` with no selector. `App` reads only `panel`, `connection` (through `surfaceOf`) and `decks`, but it re-renders, and with it every unmemoised child, on every write: each `applyInstanceId` after a `'live'`, and each poll write, which carries a new `decks` array. Select the three fields with `useShallow`, keeping the one-mount poller. This is the root half of the architecture report's F8 [`ui/src/App.tsx:57`, `ui/src/state/systemState.ts:244`]
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **`styles/surfaces.ts` is dead code.** Nothing but `surfaces.test.ts` imports `SURFACE_RAMP`, `stepsExactlyOne`, `nextSurface` or `surfaceVar`. The header calls it "a MECHANISM PLUS ITS PROOF" checked against `tokens.css` "IN THIS ORDER" by `token-usage.test.ts`, which is deleted, so the copy can drift unnoticed. Delete the module and its test, and the pointer to it in `tokens.css` [`ui/src/styles/surfaces.ts`, `ui/src/styles/surfaces.test.ts`, `ui/src/styles/tokens.css:81-82`]
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **`visually-hidden.css` and `index.css` name three consumers, and the class has eight.** Both list `CardDetail`, `ManaCurve` and `SkipLink`. It is also used in `AgentViewsNav`, `ConnectionPill`, `DeckAnnouncer`, `GroupsView` and `TierListView`. The file also says only the `CardDetail` announcement needs `pointer-events: none`, but `ConnectionPill`'s and `DeckAnnouncer`'s live regions are the same kind of 1px region with no such rule. Describe the consumers by role, without a list, and make the three live regions follow one rule: drop `CardDetail`'s if `clip-path: inset(50%)` already removes hit-testing (check in a real browser), or add it to the other two [`ui/src/styles/visually-hidden.css:3-6`, `:26-30`, `ui/src/index.css:31-33`, `ui/src/containers/CardDetail/CardDetailChrome.css:218-220`]
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **Line citations and counts in the shared styles have drifted.** `card-geometry.css` cites `DESIGN.md:362` for the card-geometry sentence (now at `:673`; `:362` is frontmatter) and `DESIGN.md:389` for the loading well (now a tier-list comment). It says "Four components draw this rectangle", and `index.css` lists four; `card-shape` is used in seven (`CardPlaceholder`, `CardDetail`, `CardTile`, `GroupsView`, `SuggestionsView`, `SwapsView`, `TierListView`). `index.css` still calls the app "the placeholder shell". Cite section names and describe consumers by role (F1's patch) [`ui/src/styles/card-geometry.css:4`, `:11`, `:44`, `ui/src/index.css:12-13`, `:26-28`]
- [ ] [Review][Patch] **The left slot's comment sits inside the ternary's condition.** After formatting it reads `surface.kind ===` / `'booting' /* six-line comment */ ? null : surface.panel === 'no-active-deck' ? …`, so the booting arm is hard to find and easy to break in an edit. Move the choice into a small function with early returns, each comment beside its arm [`ui/src/App.tsx:173-195`]

**Rejected**

The only helper that saved a test was `_assert_bundles_match` (`tests/integration/test_build_plugin.py:112`), and it does assert.

Recommended: (a) for `test_upsert_duplicate_cards` and `test_empty_import`, whose behaviour lives in the orchestrator (B3's zero-card patch); (b) for the batching test.

Four tests (`test_schema.py:129`, `:147`, `:167`, and `test_index_builder.py:239`) exercise sqlite-vec itself, so they keep passing whatever happens to `hybrid_search`'s KNN, filter or JOIN. Options: (a) delete the four and keep the `create_card_vec_table` shape tests; (b) re-point them at `hybrid_search` and `build_card_embeddings`; (c) keep them as sqlite-vec contract pins, with one shared `_knn`. [`tests/unit/data/test_database.py`] Options: (a) delete the two `event.contains` tests and move the recording-cursor cases to real `sqlite3` with a trace callback, like `:491-509`; (b) delete the `event.contains` tests only; (c) leave them.
